</button></div></form></div> <script>// TODO: Move to external file after testing.
    let searchButton = document.querySelector('.nav-search-button');
    let searchWrapper = document.querySelector('#nav-site-search');
    let icon = document.querySelector('i');
    let siteHeaderWrap = document.querySelector('.site-header');
    let navSponsor = document.querySelector('#nav-partner-logos');

    searchButton.addEventListener('click', () => toggleSearch());
    
    const toggleSearch = () => {
        icon.classList.toggle('saracens-font-icon--search');
        icon.classList.toggle('saracens-font-icon--cross');
        searchButton.classList.toggle('rotate-45');

        // find the search wrapper toggle hidden class
        searchWrapper.classList.toggle('hidden-search');

        // hide the navigation sponsor
        navSponsor.classList.toggle('hide');

        // set background colour of header
        siteHeaderWrap.classList.toggle('search-wrapper-active'); 
    }

    // if page is scrolled, hide the search bar
    window.addEventListener('scroll', () => {
        if(window.scrollY > 100) {
            // toggle the magnifying glass
            if(searchButton.classList.contains('rotate-45')) {
                searchButton.classList.remove('rotate-45');
                icon.classList.remove('saracens-font-icon--cross');
                icon.classList.add('saracens-font-icon--search');

                navSponsor.classList.remove('hide');
                siteHeaderWrap.classList.remove('search-wrapper-active');
            }
            // hide the search bar
            if(!searchWrapper.classList.contains('hidden-search')) searchWrapper.classList.add('hidden-search');
            if(!searchWrapper.classList.contains('search-wrapper-active')) siteHeaderWrap.classList.remove('search-wrapper-active');
        }
    })</script><div class="site-inner"><div class="content-sidebar-wrap"><main class="content" id="genesis-content"><article class="post-17 page type-page status-publish entry"><div class="entry-content"><div class="vc_row wpb_row vc_row-fluid"><div class="rev_slider_wrapper" style="overflow: visible; height: 541px;"><div class="saracens-desktop-slider multiple-slides revslider-initialised tp-simpleresponsive" data-height="900" data-slideactive="rs-736950" data-version="5.0" id="revslider-12" style="margin-top: 0px; margin-bottom: 0px; height: 541px;"><ul class="tp-revslider-mainul" style="visibility: visible; display: block; overflow: hidden; width: 770px; height: 100%; max-height: none; left: 0px;"><li class="fixture-slide-template align-left tp-revslider-slidesli active-revslide" data-transition="fade" style="perspective: 6000px; width: 100%; height: 100%; overflow: hidden; z-index: 20; visibility: inherit; opacity: 1; background-color: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0);">

<script>jQuery(document).ready(function($) {
        let filterWrap = document.querySelector('#fixtures-filter-wrap');
        filterWrap.addEventListener('click', () => toggleFilters());

        const toggleFilters = () => {
            let filterContent = filterWrap.querySelector('.filter-wrap-content');

            filterContent.classList.toggle('filter-wrap-content--hide');
            filterContent.classList.toggle('filter-wrap-content--show');
            filterWrap.classList.toggle('filter-wrap-content--closed');
            filterWrap.classList.toggle('filter-wrap-content--open');

            // Toggle chevron/cross icon
            filterWrap.classList.toggle('cross-icon');
            filterWrap.classList.toggle('chevron-down-icon');
        }

        /**
         * Fixture/league toggle.
         */
        let fixtureButtons = $('#toggle-fixture-style-buttons [data-display]');

        const toggleFixtureBlock = (e) => {
            let blockId = $(e.currentTarget).data('display');

            if ($(blockId).is(':visible')) {
                return;
            }

            $(fixtureButtons).removeClass('active');
            $(e.currentTarget).addClass('active');

            $('#fixtures-display, #league-display').filter(':visible').stop().fadeOut(300, function() {
                $(blockId).stop().fadeIn(300);
            });
        };

        fixtureButtons.on('click', toggleFixtureBlock);
    });</script> <template id="fixture_block_played"><div class="slide"><div class="fixture fixture-carousel-item text-center fixture-first last-fixture" data-daterange="0" data-events="" data-sports="" data-teams="" data-venues="" id="fixture-_"><div class="fixture-wrap match-played"><div class="row fixture-title"><div class="col-xs-6 col-sm-6 col-md-6"><h6 class="heading-light uppercase text-left"></h6></div><div class="col-xs-6 col-sm-6 col-md-6 text-right"><h6 class="heading-light uppercase"></h6></div><div class="divider"></div></div><div class="row fixture-dates text-center"><div class="col-xs-6 col-sm-6 col-md-6 text-right"><h4 class="heading-light uppercase">Thu 1 <span class="fw-100">Jan</span></h4></div><div class="col-xs-6 col-sm-6 col-md-6 text-left border-left"><h4 class="heading-light uppercase">KO <span class="fw-100"> 00H00</span></h4></div></div><div class="row teams"><div class="col-xs-5 image-container">

<span>View more<div class="border border--red"></div></span>
</button></div></div></div></section> <script>jQuery(document).ready(function($) {
        const classes = { hide: 'hide' };
        let posts = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('.post'));
        let viewMoreNewsPosts = document.querySelector('.view-more-posts > button');
        const pagination = {resultsPerRow: 4, currentResults: [], currNumOfResults: '', previousResults: [], activeFilter: '', totalNumOfResults: posts.length};

        /**
        * Initial pagination set up.
        */
        const initPostPagination = () => {
            pagination.currentResults = posts.slice(0, pagination.resultsPerRow);
            pagination.currNumOfResults = pagination.currentResults.length;

            showNewsPosts(false, posts);
            showNewsPosts(true, pagination.currentResults);

            // Add event listener to the view more btn
            viewMoreNewsPosts.addEventListener('click', () => loadMoreNewsPosts());

            if(pagination.currNumOfResults === pagination.totalNumOfResults) {
                viewMoreNewsPosts.classList.add(classes.hide);
            }

            //applyAlternateStylesToPosts();
        }

        /**
        * Load next set of results.
        */
        const loadMoreNewsPosts = () => {
            let newResultLength = pagination.currNumOfResults + pagination.resultsPerRow;
            pagination.previousResults = pagination.currentResults;
            pagination.currentResults = posts.slice(0, newResultLength);
            pagination.currNumOfResults = pagination.currentResults.length;

            showNewsPosts(false, pagination.previousResults);
            showNewsPosts(true, pagination.currentResults);

            // hide the view more button if we have reached max number of results
            if(pagination.currNumOfResults === pagination.totalNumOfResults) {
                viewMoreNewsPosts.classList.add(classes.hide);
            }

            //applyAlternateStylesToPosts();

        }

        /**
        * Show or hide news posts.
        * @param {Boolean} show Show or hide the posts.
        * @param {Array} posts An array of posts.
        */
        const showNewsPosts = (show, posts) => {
            posts.forEach(post => {
                if(show) {
                    if(post.classList.contains(classes.hide)) {
                        post.classList.remove(classes.hide);
                    }
                } else {
                    post.classList.add(classes.hide);
                }
            });
        }

        initPostPagination();

        /**
        * Add event listeners to the social icon toggles.
        */
        const addEventListenerToSocialToggles = () => {
            let toggles = document.querySelectorAll('.post-social-toggle');
            toggles.forEach(toggle => toggle.addEventListener('click', (e) =>  helpers.toggleSocialIcons(e)));
        }

        addEventListenerToSocialToggles();
    });</script> <div class="wpb_column vc_column_container vc_col-sm-12"><div class="vc_column-inner"><div class="wpb_wrapper"></div></div></div></div><div class="vc_row wpb_row vc_row-fluid white-background">
